   502	
   503	    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
   504	        super.onDraw(canvas)
   505	
   506	        val progress = bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis)
   507	        val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   508	            progress,
   509	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   510	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   511	        )
   512	        val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2500, 3900)
   513	        drawIntroBackground(canvas, blackToPurple, purpleToSettle)
   514	
   515	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return
   516	        finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded()
   517	        finishMockCreateAvatarCarouselIfNeeded()
   518	        finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded()
   519	        val mockProgress = mockSignInProgress()
   520	        updateMockFormFocusAnimation()
   521	        updateMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   522	        updateMockInputDimAnimation()
   523	        val formFocusMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
   524	
   525	        val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(progress, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   526	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   527	            drawSettlingLogo(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, progress, logoAlpha, formFocusMotion)
   528	        }
   529	
   530	        val avatarAlpha = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   531	        val mockMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockProgress)
   532	        if (avatarAlpha > 0.01f) {
   533	            val stackAlpha = avatarAlpha * (1f - mockMotion)
   534	            val y = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha) + MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * mockMotion
   535	            val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
   536	            if (stackAlpha > 0.01f) {
   537	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveAvatar, Bounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   538	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinAvatar, Bounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   539	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyAvatar, Bounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   540	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestAvatar, Bounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   541	
   542	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveName, Bounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   543	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinName, Bounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   544	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyName, Bounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   545	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestName, Bounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   546	                drawAccountPrompt(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, y, stackAlpha)
   547	            }
   548	        }
   549	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   550	            drawMockSignInForm(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, mockMotion, formFocusMotion)
   551	        }
   552	
   553	        if (
   554	            progress < 1f ||
   555	            isMockSignInAnimating() ||
   556	            isMockFormLabelAnimating() ||
   557	            isMockFormFocusAnimating() ||
   558	            isMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimating() ||
   559	            isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating() ||
   560	            isMockForgotPasswordSubmissionAnimating() ||
   561	            isMockInputDimAnimating()
   562	        ) {
   563	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   564	        }
   565	    }
   566	
   567	    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
   568	        return when (event.actionMasked) {
   569	            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
   570	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   571	                    mockTouchDownX = event.x
   572	                    mockTouchDownY = event.y
   573	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   574	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   575	                    val touchedDropdownOptions = mockDropdownOptionsHit(event.x, event.y)
   576	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = touchedDropdownOptions?.takeIf { mockDropdownCanScroll(it) }
   577	                    mockDropdownScrollStartY = event.y
   578	                    mockDropdownScrollStartOffset = activeMockDropdownScroll?.let { mockDropdownScrollOffset(it) } ?: 0
   579	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = if (touchedDropdownOptions != null) {
   580	                        mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   581	                    } else {
   582	                        null
   583	                    }
   584	                    pressedMockDropdown = if (pressedMockDropdownOption == null && touchedDropdownOptions == null) {
   585	                        mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   586	                    } else {
   587	                        null
   588	                    }
   589	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = if (
   590	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   591	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   592	                        pressedMockDropdown == null
   593	                    ) {
   594	                        mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   595	                    } else {
   596	                        null
   597	                    }
   598	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   599	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   600	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   601	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   602	                        mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   603	                    val touchedCreateAvatarCarousel = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   604	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   605	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   606	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   607	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   608	                        mockCreateAvatarCarouselHit(event.x, event.y) &&
   609	                        !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
   610	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   611	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   612	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   613	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = if (
   614	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   615	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   616	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   617	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   618	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   619	                        touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   620	                    ) {
   621	                        mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   622	                    } else {
   623	                        null
   624	                    }
   625	                    pressedMockField = if (
   626	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   627	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   628	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   629	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   630	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   631	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   632	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   633	                    ) {
   634	                        mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   635	                    } else {
   636	                        null
   637	                    }
   638	                    pressedRememberMe = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   639	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   640	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   641	                        pressedMockField == null &&
   642	                        mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   643	                    pressedBackChevron = pressedMockField == null &&
   644	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   645	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   646	                        !pressedRememberMe &&
   647	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   648	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   649	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   650	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   651	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   652	                        mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   653	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   654	                    return true
   655	                }
   656	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   657	                pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   658	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   659	                    !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   660	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   661	                pressedAvatarProfile = if (
   662	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   663	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   664	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt
   665	                ) {
   666	                    null
   667	                } else {
   668	                    settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   669	                }
   670	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   671	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   672	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   673	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   674	            }
   675	            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
   676	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   677	                    if (updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event)) {
   678	                        return true
   679	                    }
   680	                    if (updateMockDropdownScroll(event)) {
   681	                        return true
   682	                    }
   683	                    if (mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) == null && shouldStartMockDragReturn(event)) {
   684	                        mockDragReturnInProgress = true
   685	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   686	                    }
   687	                    return true
   688	                }
   689	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   690	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   691	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   692	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   693	            }
   694	            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
   695	                if (mockDragReturnInProgress) {
   696	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   697	                    pressedMockField = null
   698	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   699	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   700	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   701	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   702	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   703	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   704	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   705	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   706	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   707	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   708	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   709	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   710	                    return true
   711	                }
   712	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   713	                    val releasedDropdownOption = mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   714	                    val releasedDropdown = mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   715	                    val releasedField = mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   716	                    val releasedRememberMe = mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   717	                    val releasedBackChevron = mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   718	                    val releasedCreateAvatarNav = mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   719	                    val releasedForgotRecoveryTarget = mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   720	                    val releasedForgotPasswordSubmit = mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   721	                    val wasTouchingDropdownOptions = activeMockDropdownScroll != null ||
   722	                        mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) != null
   723	                    val wasScrollingDropdown = mockDropdownScrollMoved
   724	                    val wasPressingRememberMe = pressedRememberMe
   725	                    val wasPressingBackChevron = pressedBackChevron
   726	                    val wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget != null
   727	                    val wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit
   728	                    val wasDraggingCreateAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag
   729	                    val createAvatarDragMoved = mockCreateAvatarDragMoved
   730	                    val createAvatarDragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
   731	                    val createAvatarDragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress()
   732	                    val pressedDropdown = pressedMockDropdown
   733	                    val pressedOption = pressedMockDropdownOption
   734	                    val pressedCreateAvatarNav = pressedMockCreateAvatarNav
   735	                    val pressedForgotRecoverySelection = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget
   736	                    val shouldSelectDropdownOption = !wasScrollingDropdown &&
   737	                        pressedOption != null &&
   738	                        pressedOption == releasedDropdownOption
   739	                    val shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null &&
   740	                        pressedForgotRecoverySelection == releasedForgotRecoveryTarget
   741	                    val shouldToggleDropdown = pressedDropdown != null && pressedDropdown == releasedDropdown
   742	                    val shouldToggleRememberMe = wasPressingRememberMe && releasedRememberMe
   743	                    val shouldCloseMockSignIn = wasPressingBackChevron && releasedBackChevron
   744	                    val shouldSubmitForgotPassword = wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit && releasedForgotPasswordSubmit
   745	                    val shouldNavigateCreateAvatar = pressedCreateAvatarNav != null &&
   746	                        !createAvatarDragMoved &&
   747	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == releasedCreateAvatarNav
   748	                    val shouldSwipeCreateAvatar = wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
   749	                        createAvatarDragMoved &&
   750	                        createAvatarDragDirection != null &&
   751	                        createAvatarDragProgress >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_DRAG_COMMIT_PROGRESS
   752	                    val shouldFocusField = releasedField != null &&
   753	                        !wasTouchingDropdownOptions &&
   754	                        !wasPressingRememberMe &&
   755	                        !wasPressingBackChevron &&
   756	                        !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget &&
   757	                        !wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   758	                        !wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
   759	                        pressedDropdown == null &&
   760	                        pressedOption == null &&
   761	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   762	                        (pressedMockField == null || pressedMockField == releasedField)
   763	                    pressedMockField = null
   764	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   765	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   766	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   767	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   768	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   769	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   770	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   771	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   772	                    this.pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   773	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   774	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   775	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   776	                    super.performClick()
   777	                    if (shouldCloseMockSignIn) {
   778	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   779	                    } else if (shouldSwipeCreateAvatar && createAvatarDragDirection != null) {
   780	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   781	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   782	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(createAvatarDragDirection, createAvatarDragProgress)
   783	                    } else if (shouldNavigateCreateAvatar && pressedCreateAvatarNav != null) {
   784	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   785	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   786	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(pressedCreateAvatarNav)
   787	                    } else if (wasDraggingCreateAvatar) {
   788	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   789	                    } else if (shouldSelectDropdownOption && pressedOption != null) {
   790	                        setMockDropdownValue(pressedOption)
   791	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   792	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   793	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   794	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   795	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   796	                    } else if (shouldToggleDropdown && pressedDropdown != null) {
   797	                        expandedMockDropdown = if (expandedMockDropdown == pressedDropdown) null else pressedDropdown
   798	                        expandedMockDropdown?.let { prepareMockDropdownScroll(it) }
   799	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   800	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   801	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   802	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   803	                    } else if (shouldToggleRememberMe) {
   804	                        rememberMeChecked = !rememberMeChecked
   805	                        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
   806	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   807	                    } else if (shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget && pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null) {
   808	                        setMockForgotRecoveryTarget(pressedForgotRecoverySelection)
   809	                    } else if (shouldSubmitForgotPassword) {
   810	                        startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
   811	                    } else if (shouldFocusField && releasedField != null) {
   812	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   813	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   814	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   815	                        focusMockSignInField(releasedField)
   816	                    } else if (wasTouchingDropdownOptions || wasScrollingDropdown) {
   817	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   818	                    } else if (!wasPressingRememberMe && !wasPressingBackChevron && !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget) {
   819	                        if (expandedMockDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
   820	                            expandedMockDropdown = null
   821	                            restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   822	                            restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   823	                        }
   824	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   825	                    } else {
   826	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   827	                    }
   828	                    return true
   829	                }
   830	                val shouldOpenMockCreateAccount = pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   831	                    settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   832	                val shouldOpenMockSignIn = pressedSignInPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   833	                val shouldOpenMockForgotPassword = pressedForgotPasswordPrompt &&
   834	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   835	                val releasedAvatarProfile = settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   836	                val shouldNavigate = pressedAvatarProfile != null && pressedAvatarProfile == releasedAvatarProfile
   837	                clickAvatarProfile = pressedAvatarProfile
   838	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
   839	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   840	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
   841	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   842	                if (shouldOpenMockCreateAccount) {
   843	                    super.performClick()
   844	                    openMockCreateAccountScreen()
   845	                    true
   846	                } else if (shouldOpenMockSignIn) {
   847	                    super.performClick()
   848	                    openMockSignInScreen()
   849	                    true
   850	                } else if (shouldOpenMockForgotPassword) {
   851	                    super.performClick()
   852	                    openMockForgotPasswordScreen()
   853	                    true
   854	                } else if (shouldNavigate) {
   855	                    performClick()
   856	                    true
   857	                } else {
   858	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
   859	                    false
   860	                }
   861	            }
   862	            MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
   863	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
   864	                clickAvatarProfile = null
   865	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   866	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
   867	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   868	                pressedMockField = null
   869	                pressedRememberMe = false
   870	                pressedBackChevron = false
   871	                pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   872	                activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   873	                mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   874	                mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   875	                pressedMockDropdown = null
   876	                pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   877	                pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   878	                pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   879	                activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   880	                mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   881	                mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   882	                false
   883	            }
   884	            else -> pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   885	                pressedSignInPrompt ||
   886	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   887	                pressedAvatarProfile != null ||
   888	                mockSignInStartMillis != null
   889	        }
   890	    }
   891	
   892	    override fun onCheckIsTextEditor(): Boolean {
   893	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && !isMockSignInClosing()
   894	    }
   895	
   896	    override fun onCreateInputConnection(outAttrs: EditorInfo): InputConnection? {
   897	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return null
   898	        outAttrs.inputType = when (field) {
   899	            MockSignInField.Password,
   900	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_PASSWORD
   901	            MockSignInField.Email -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_EMAIL_ADDRESS
   902	            MockSignInField.Month,
   903	            MockSignInField.Day,
   904	            MockSignInField.Year -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_NUMBER
   905	            else -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_NORMAL
   906	        }
   907	        outAttrs.imeOptions = if (field == activeMockFields().lastOrNull()) {
   908	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE
   909	        } else {
   910	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_NEXT
   911	        }
   912	        val textLength = mockSignInFieldText(field).length
   913	        outAttrs.initialSelStart = textLength
   914	        outAttrs.initialSelEnd = textLength
   915	        return MockSignInInputConnection()
   916	    }
   917	
   918	    override fun onKeyDown(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
   919	        if (focusedMockField != null) {
   920	            return handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode, event) || super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
   921	        }
   922	        return super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
   923	    }
   924	
   925	    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
   926	    override fun onKeyMultiple(keyCode: Int, repeatCount: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
   927	        val characters = event.getCharacters()
   928	        if (focusedMockField != null && !characters.isNullOrEmpty()) {
   929	            return appendMockSignInInput(characters)
   930	        }
   931	        return super.onKeyMultiple(keyCode, repeatCount, event)
   932	    }
   933	
   934	    override fun performClick(): Boolean {
   935	        super.performClick()
   936	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return false
   937	        val profile = clickAvatarProfile ?: return false
   938	        onAvatarSelected?.invoke(profile)
   939	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   940	        return true
   941	    }
   942	
   943	    private fun drawSettlingLogo(
   944	        canvas: Canvas,
   945	        stageLeft: Float,
   946	        stageTop: Float,
   947	        stageScale: Float,
   948	        progress: Float,
   949	        alpha: Float,
   950	        formFocusMotion: Float
   951	    ) {
   952	        val logoEntry = bouncySegmentMs(progress, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
   953	        val finalProgress = easedSegmentMs(progress, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   954	        val focusShiftY = mockLogoFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
   955	        val base = offsetBounds(Bounds(222f, 150f, 750f, 535f), focusShiftY)
   956	        val final = offsetBounds(Bounds(297f, LOGO_FINAL_TOP, 600f, LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT), focusShiftY)
   957	
   958	        if (logoEntry >= 0.999f) {
   959	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoCombined, lerpBounds(base, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
   960	            return
   961	        }
   962	
   963	        val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(447f, 1037f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
   964	        val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(453f, -417f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
   965	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoSimple, lerpBounds(simpleEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
   966	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoEyes, lerpBounds(eyesEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
   967	    }
   968	
   969	    private fun drawIntroBackground(canvas: Canvas, solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float) {
   970	        val standardSignInBackground = standardSignInBackgroundForSize()
   971	        if (gradientProgress <= 0.001f) {
   972	            canvas.drawColor(lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress))
   973	            return
   974	        }
   975	
   976	        val solid = lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress)
   977	        backgroundPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
   978	            0f,
   979	            0f,
   980	            0f,
   981	            height.toFloat(),
   982	            intArrayOf(
   983	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_TOP, gradientProgress),
   984	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_CENTER, gradientProgress),
   985	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_BOTTOM, gradientProgress)
   986	            ),
   987	            null,
   988	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
   989	        )
   990	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
   991	        backgroundPaint.shader = null
   992	
   993	        if (standardSignInBackground != null) {
   994	            drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas, gradientProgress)
   995	            drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas, standardSignInBackground, gradientProgress)
   996	        }
   997	    }
   998	
   999	    private fun drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas: Canvas, alpha: Float) {
  1000	        backgroundPaint.color = COLOR_FRAME_1
  1001	        backgroundPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1002	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1003	        backgroundPaint.alpha = 255
  1004	    }
  1005	
  1006	    private fun drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas: Canvas, bitmap: Bitmap, alpha: Float) {
  1007	        tempRect.set(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat())
  1008	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * STANDARD_BACKGROUND_SETTLED_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1009	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1010	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1011	    }
  1012	
  1013	    private fun drawFigmaBitmap(
  1014	        canvas: Canvas,
  1015	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  1016	        bounds: Bounds,
  1017	        stageLeft: Float,
  1018	        stageTop: Float,
  1019	        stageScale: Float,
  1020	        alpha: Float
  1021	    ) {
  1022	        tempRect.set(
  1023	            stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale,
  1024	            stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale,
  1025	            stageLeft + (bounds.x + bounds.w) * stageScale,
  1026	            stageTop + (bounds.y + bounds.h) * stageScale
  1027	        )
  1028	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1029	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1030	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1031	    }
  1032	
  1033	    private fun drawAccountPrompt(
  1034	        canvas: Canvas,
  1035	        stageLeft: Float,
  1036	        stageTop: Float,
  1037	        stageScale: Float,
  1038	        yOffset: Float,
  1039	        alpha: Float
  1040	    ) {
  1041	        val isLandscape = width > height
  1042	        val textSize = signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale
  1043	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1044	        accountPromptPaint.textSize = textSize
  1045	        formTitlePaint.textSize = textSize
  1046	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1047	        formTitlePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1048	
  1049	        val accountPromptShiftY = if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1050	        val centerY = stageTop +
  1051	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + accountPromptShiftY + yOffset) * stageScale
  1052	        val secondaryMetrics = accountPromptPaint.fontMetrics
  1053	        val primaryMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1054	        val secondaryBaselineY = centerY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1055	        val primaryBaselineY = centerY - (primaryMetrics.ascent + primaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1056	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1057	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1058	            canvas.drawText(
  1059	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
  1060	                stageLeft + accountPromptCreateCenterX() * stageScale,
  1061	                secondaryBaselineY,
  1062	                accountPromptPaint
  1063	            )
  1064	        }
  1065	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn) {
  1066	            formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1067	            canvas.drawText(
  1068	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1069	                stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale,
  1070	                primaryBaselineY,
  1071	                formTitlePaint
  1072	            )
  1073	        }
  1074	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  1075	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1076	            canvas.drawText(
  1077	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
  1078	                stageLeft + accountPromptForgotCenterX() * stageScale,
  1079	                secondaryBaselineY,
  1080	                accountPromptPaint
  1081	            )
  1082	        }
  1083	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = 255
  1084	        accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1085	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1086	    }
  1087	
  1088	    private fun drawMockSignInForm(
  1089	        canvas: Canvas,
  1090	        stageLeft: Float,
  1091	        stageTop: Float,
  1092	        stageScale: Float,
  1093	        progress: Float,
  1094	        formFocusMotion: Float
  1095	    ) {
  1096	        updateMockFormLabelAnimation()
  1097	
  1098	        val alpha = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1099	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1100	            drawMockCreateAccountForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1101	            return
  1102	        }
  1103	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  1104	            drawMockForgotPasswordForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1105	            return
  1106	        }
  1107	
  1108	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha)
  1109	        val fieldFillAlpha = (alpha * 26f).roundToInt()
  1110	        val fieldStrokeAlpha = (alpha * 185f).roundToInt()
  1111	        val fieldX = mockFormX()
  1112	        val usernameY = MOCK_FORM_Y + yOffset
  1113	        val passwordY = usernameY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_GAP
  1114	
  1115	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fieldFillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1116	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(fieldStrokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1117	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1118	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1119	
  1120	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1121	        val rememberMeY = passwordY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TOP_GAP + focusShiftY
  1122	        drawMockSignInTitle(canvas, usernameY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1123	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Username, fieldX, usernameY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1124	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Password, fieldX, passwordY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1125	        drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  1126	            canvas,
  1127	            fieldX,
  1128	            rememberMeY,
  1129	            stageLeft,
  1130	            stageTop,
  1131	            stageScale,
  1132	            alpha
  1133	        )
  1134	        drawBackChevron(
  1135	            canvas,
  1136	            fieldX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f,
  1137	            rememberMeY + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_TOP_GAP,
  1138	            stageLeft,
  1139	            stageTop,
  1140	            stageScale,
  1141	            alpha
  1142	        )
  1143	        drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1144	            canvas,
  1145	            stageLeft,
  1146	            stageTop,
  1147	            stageScale,
  1148	            alpha,
  1149	            yOffset,
  1150	            focusShiftY
  1151	        )
  1152	
  1153	        formLabelPaint.alpha = 255
  1154	        formInputPaint.alpha = 255
  1155	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1156	        formCaretPaint.alpha = 255
  1157	    }
  1158	
  1159	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountForm(
  1160	        canvas: Canvas,
  1161	        stageLeft: Float,
  1162	        stageTop: Float,
  1163	        stageScale: Float,
  1164	        alpha: Float,
  1165	        formFocusMotion: Float
  1166	    ) {
  1167	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha)
  1168	        val fieldFillAlpha = (alpha * 26f).roundToInt()
  1169	        val fieldStrokeAlpha = (alpha * 185f).roundToInt()
  1170	        val fieldX = mockCreateFormX()
  1171	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1172	
  1173	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fieldFillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1174	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(fieldStrokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1175	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1176	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1177	
  1178	        val firstFieldY = MOCK_FORM_Y + yOffset + focusShiftY
  1179	        drawMockCreateAccountTitle(canvas, firstFieldY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1180	        activeMockFields().forEach { field ->
  1181	            drawMockSignInField(
  1182	                canvas,
  1183	                field,
  1184	                mockActiveFieldX(field),
  1185	                mockActiveFieldY(field, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1186	                stageLeft,
  1187	                stageTop,
  1188	                stageScale,
  1189	                alpha,
  1190	                mockActiveFieldWidth(field)
  1191	            )
  1192	        }
  1193	        drawMockDropdownControl(

  1356	
  1357	    private fun drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1358	        canvas: Canvas,
  1359	        stageLeft: Float,
  1360	        stageTop: Float,
  1361	        stageScale: Float,
  1362	        alpha: Float,
  1363	        yOffset: Float,
  1364	        focusShiftY: Float
  1365	    ) {
  1366	        val dimProgress = mockInputDimProgress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1367	        if (dimProgress <= 0.01f) return
  1368	
  1369	        inputDimPaint.alpha = (dimProgress * MOCK_INPUT_DIM_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1370	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), inputDimPaint)
  1371	        inputDimPaint.alpha = 255
  1372	
  1373	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 26f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1374	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 185f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1375	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1376	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1377	
  1378	        val activeDropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: mockInputDimActiveDropdown
  1379	        if (activeDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1380	            drawMockDropdownControl(
  1381	                canvas,
  1382	                activeDropdown,
  1383	                mockDropdownX(activeDropdown),
  1384	                mockDropdownY(activeDropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1385	                stageLeft,
  1386	                stageTop,
  1387	                stageScale,
  1388	                alpha,
  1389	                mockDropdownWidth(activeDropdown)
  1390	            )
  1391	            if (expandedMockDropdown == activeDropdown) {
  1392	                drawMockDropdownOptions(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1393	            }
  1394	            return
  1395	        }
  1396	
  1397	        val activeField = focusedMockField ?: mockInputDimActiveField ?: return
  1398	        drawMockSignInField(
  1399	            canvas,
  1400	            activeField,
  1401	            mockActiveFieldX(activeField),
  1402	            mockActiveFieldY(activeField, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1403	            stageLeft,
  1404	            stageTop,
  1405	            stageScale,
  1406	            alpha,
  1407	            mockActiveFieldWidth(activeField)
  1408	        )
  1409	    }

  1411	    private fun drawMockSignInTitle(
  1412	        canvas: Canvas,
  1413	        fieldTopY: Float,
  1414	        stageLeft: Float,
  1415	        stageTop: Float,
  1416	        stageScale: Float,
  1417	        alpha: Float
  1418	    ) {
  1419	        val motion = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1420	        val sourceTextSize = signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale
  1421	        val targetTextSize = MOCK_FORM_TITLE_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1422	        val sourceCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1423	        val targetCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1424	        val sourceCenterY = stageTop +
  1425	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()) * stageScale
  1426	        formTitlePaint.textSize = sourceTextSize
  1427	        val sourceMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1428	        val sourceBaselineY = sourceCenterY - (sourceMetrics.ascent + sourceMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1429	        val targetBaselineY = stageTop + (fieldTopY - MOCK_FORM_TITLE_BASELINE_GAP) * stageScale
  1430	
  1431	        formTitlePaint.textSize = lerpFloat(sourceTextSize, targetTextSize, motion)
  1432	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1433	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1434	        canvas.drawText(
  1435	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1436	            lerpFloat(sourceCenterX, targetCenterX, motion),
  1437	            lerpFloat(sourceBaselineY, targetBaselineY, motion),
  1438	            formTitlePaint
  1439	        )
  1440	    }

  1503	
  1504	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButton(
  1505	        canvas: Canvas,
  1506	        x: Float,
  1507	        y: Float,
  1508	        stageLeft: Float,
  1509	        stageTop: Float,
  1510	        stageScale: Float,
  1511	        alpha: Float,
  1512	        buttonWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  1513	    ) {
  1514	        tempRect.set(
  1515	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  1516	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  1517	            stageLeft + (x + buttonWidth) * stageScale,
  1518	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  1519	        )
  1520	        val radius = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_RADIUS * stageScale
  1521	        val submissionState = forgotPasswordSubmissionState
  1522	        val enabled = mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  1523	        val pressed = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit && enabled
  1524	        val fillAlpha = when {
  1525	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 86f
  1526	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 72f
  1527	            pressed -> 92f
  1528	            enabled -> 68f
  1529	            else -> 18f
  1530	        }
  1531	        val strokeAlpha = when {
  1532	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 245f
  1533	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 228f
  1534	            pressed -> 238f
  1535	            enabled -> 210f
  1536	            else -> 118f
  1537	        }
  1538	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  1539	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * fillAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1540	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * strokeAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1541	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1542	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1543	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1544	        if (submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  1545	            drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(canvas, tempRect, radius, alpha)
  1546	        }
  1547	
  1548	        formTitlePaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1549	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1550	        val metrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1551	        val baselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  1552	        val maxTextWidth = buttonWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  1553	        val showCheck = submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  1554	        val checkSize = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_SIZE * stageScale else 0f
  1555	        val checkGap = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_GAP * stageScale else 0f
  1556	        val textMaxWidth = (maxTextWidth - checkSize - checkGap).coerceAtLeast(maxTextWidth * 0.5f)
  1557	        val buttonText = trailingFittingText(
  1558	            mockForgotPasswordButtonText(),
  1559	            if (showCheck) textMaxWidth else maxTextWidth,
  1560	            formTitlePaint
  1561	        )
  1562	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * when {
  1563	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 226f
  1564	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 255f
  1565	            enabled -> 255f
  1566	            else -> 138f
  1567	        }).roundToInt()
  1568	        formTitlePaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1569	        val textX = if (showCheck) {
  1570	            val textWidth = formTitlePaint.measureText(buttonText)
  1571	            val groupWidth = checkSize + checkGap + textWidth
  1572	            val groupLeft = tempRect.centerX() - groupWidth / 2f
  1573	            val checkLeft = groupLeft
  1574	            val checkCenterY = tempRect.centerY()
  1575	            backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1576	            backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 2.1f * stageScale
  1577	            canvas.drawLine(
  1578	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.18f,
  1579	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.03f,
  1580	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  1581	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  1582	                backChevronPaint
  1583	            )
  1584	            canvas.drawLine(
  1585	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  1586	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  1587	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.84f,
  1588	                checkCenterY - checkSize * 0.28f,
  1589	                backChevronPaint
  1590	            )
  1591	            groupLeft + checkSize + checkGap + textWidth / 2f
  1592	        } else {
  1593	            tempRect.centerX()
  1594	        }
  1595	        canvas.drawText(
  1596	            buttonText,
  1597	            textX,
  1598	            baselineY,
  1599	            formTitlePaint
  1600	        )
  1601	    }
  1602	
  1603	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(
  1604	        canvas: Canvas,
  1605	        buttonRect: RectF,
  1606	        radius: Float,
  1607	        alpha: Float
  1608	    ) {
  1609	        val progress = mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress()
  1610	        val sweepWidth = buttonRect.width() * 0.44f
  1611	        val sweepCenterX = buttonRect.left + buttonRect.width() * (progress * 1.35f - 0.18f)
  1612	        val sweepLeft = sweepCenterX - sweepWidth / 2f
  1613	        val sweepRight = sweepCenterX + sweepWidth / 2f
  1614	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  1615	            sweepLeft,
  1616	            buttonRect.top,
  1617	            sweepRight,
  1618	            buttonRect.top,
  1619	            intArrayOf(
  1620	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255),
  1621	                Color.argb((alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 54f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255),
  1622	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255)
  1623	            ),
  1624	            floatArrayOf(0f, 0.5f, 1f),
  1625	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1626	        )
  1627	        canvas.save()
  1628	        tempPath.reset()
  1629	        tempPath.addRoundRect(buttonRect, radius, radius, Path.Direction.CW)
  1630	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  1631	        canvas.drawRect(sweepLeft, buttonRect.top, sweepRight, buttonRect.bottom, formFieldFillPaint)
  1632	        canvas.restore()
  1633	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  1634	    }

  1636	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoverySelector(
  1637	        canvas: Canvas,
  1638	        x: Float,
  1639	        y: Float,
  1640	        stageLeft: Float,
  1641	        stageTop: Float,
  1642	        stageScale: Float,
  1643	        alpha: Float,
  1644	        formWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  1645	    ) {
  1646	        formLabelPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1647	        formLabelPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1648	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  1649	
  1650	        val labelWidth = formLabelPaint.measureText(MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_TEXT)
  1651	        formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_OPTION_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1652	        val optionBoxWidth = mockForgotRecoverySelectorBoxWidth(
  1653	            usernameTextWidth = formInputPaint.measureText(MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Username.displayText),
  1654	            passwordTextWidth = formInputPaint.measureText(MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password.displayText),
  1655	            baseBoxWidth = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_WIDTH * stageScale
  1656	        )
  1657	        val layout = mockForgotRecoverySelectorLayout(
  1658	            left = stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  1659	            width = formWidth * stageScale,
  1660	            labelWidth = labelWidth,
  1661	            boxWidth = optionBoxWidth
  1662	        )
  1663	        val rowTop = stageTop + y * stageScale
  1664	        val rowCenterY = rowTop + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_HEIGHT * stageScale / 2f
  1665	        val labelMetrics = formLabelPaint.fontMetrics
  1666	        canvas.drawText(
  1667	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_TEXT,
  1668	            layout.labelX,
  1669	            rowCenterY - (labelMetrics.ascent + labelMetrics.descent) / 2f,
  1670	            formLabelPaint
  1671	        )
  1672	
  1673	        drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(
  1674	            canvas,
  1675	            MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Username,
  1676	            layout.usernameBoxX,
  1677	            rowTop,
  1678	            layout.boxWidth,
  1679	            stageScale,
  1680	            alpha
  1681	        )
  1682	        drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(
  1683	            canvas,
  1684	            MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password,
  1685	            layout.passwordBoxX,
  1686	            rowTop,
  1687	            layout.boxWidth,
  1688	            stageScale,
  1689	            alpha
  1690	        )
  1691	    }
  1692	
  1693	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(
  1694	        canvas: Canvas,
  1695	        target: MockForgotRecoveryTarget,
  1696	        left: Float,
  1697	        top: Float,
  1698	        boxWidth: Float,
  1699	        stageScale: Float,
  1700	        alpha: Float
  1701	    ) {
  1702	        val right = left + boxWidth
  1703	        val bottom = top + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_HEIGHT * stageScale
  1704	        tempRect.set(left, top, right, bottom)
  1705	        val selected = forgotRecoveryTarget == target
  1706	        val pressed = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == target
  1707	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * when {
  1708	            pressed -> 82f
  1709	            selected -> 64f
  1710	            else -> 14f
  1711	        }).roundToInt()
  1712	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * when {
  1713	            pressed -> 245f
  1714	            selected -> 235f
  1715	            else -> 142f
  1716	        }).roundToInt()
  1717	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  1718	
  1719	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1720	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1721	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1722	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1723	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1724	
  1725	        formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_OPTION_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1726	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (selected || pressed) 255f else 178f).roundToInt()
  1727	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1728	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  1729	        canvas.drawText(
  1730	            target.displayText,
  1731	            tempRect.centerX(),
  1732	            tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  1733	            formInputPaint
  1734	        )
  1735	    }
  1736	
  1737	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordBodyParagraph(
  1738	        canvas: Canvas,
  1739	        text: String,
  1740	        centerX: Float,
  1741	        topY: Float,
  1742	        maxWidth: Float,
  1743	        stageLeft: Float,
  1744	        stageTop: Float,
  1745	        stageScale: Float,
  1746	        alpha: Float
  1747	    ): Float {
  1748	        val paragraphs = text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  1749	        val leadBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  1750	            canvas,
  1751	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  1752	            centerX,
  1753	            topY,
  1754	            maxWidth,
  1755	            stageLeft,
  1756	            stageTop,
  1757	            stageScale,
  1758	            alpha,
  1759	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  1760	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT
  1761	        )
  1762	        val middleText = paragraphs.getOrNull(1) ?: return leadBottomY
  1763	        val middleBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  1764	            canvas,
  1765	            middleText,
  1766	            centerX,
  1767	            leadBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  1768	            maxWidth,
  1769	            stageLeft,
  1770	            stageTop,
  1771	            stageScale,
  1772	            alpha,
  1773	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  1774	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  1775	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  1776	        )
  1777	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  1778	        if (bottomText.isEmpty()) return middleBottomY
  1779	        return drawMockFormParagraph(
  1780	            canvas,
  1781	            bottomText,
  1782	            centerX,
  1783	            middleBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  1784	            maxWidth,
  1785	            stageLeft,
  1786	            stageTop,
  1787	            stageScale,
  1788	            alpha,
  1789	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  1790	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  1791	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  1792	        )
  1793	    }
  1794	
  1795	    private fun drawMockFormParagraph(
  1796	        canvas: Canvas,
  1797	        text: String,
  1798	        centerX: Float,
  1799	        topY: Float,
  1800	        maxWidth: Float,
  1801	        stageLeft: Float,
  1802	        stageTop: Float,
  1803	        stageScale: Float,
  1804	        alpha: Float,
  1805	        textSize: Float,
  1806	        lineHeight: Float,
  1807	        textAlign: Paint.Align = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1808	    ): Float {
  1809	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize * stageScale
  1810	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 218f).roundToInt()
  1811	        formInputPaint.textAlign = textAlign
  1812	
  1813	        val lines = wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  1814	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  1815	        val x = when (textAlign) {
  1816	            Paint.Align.LEFT -> stageLeft + (centerX - maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  1817	            Paint.Align.RIGHT -> stageLeft + (centerX + maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  1818	            Paint.Align.CENTER -> stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  1819	        }
  1820	        var baselineY = stageTop + topY * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  1821	        lines.forEach { line ->
  1822	            canvas.drawText(line, x, baselineY, formInputPaint)
  1823	            baselineY += lineHeight * stageScale
  1824	        }
  1825	        return topY + lines.size * lineHeight
  1826	    }

  1828	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  1829	        canvas: Canvas,
  1830	        columnX: Float,
  1831	        firstFieldY: Float,
  1832	        stageLeft: Float,
  1833	        stageTop: Float,
  1834	        stageScale: Float,
  1835	        alpha: Float
  1836	    ) {
  1837	        val avatarScale = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  1838	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * avatarScale
  1839	        val labelBaselineFromTop = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LABEL_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * avatarScale
  1840	        val avatarTopGap = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_TOP_GAP * avatarScale
  1841	        val centerX = stageLeft + (columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * stageScale
  1842	        val baselineY = stageTop + (firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop) * stageScale
  1843	        formLabelPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * avatarScale * stageScale
  1844	        formLabelPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1845	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1846	        canvas.drawText(
  1847	            MOCK_FORM_AVATAR_TEXT,
  1848	            centerX,
  1849	            baselineY,
  1850	            formLabelPaint
  1851	        )
  1852	
  1853	        val avatarLeftX = columnX + (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - scaledAvatarSize) / 2f
  1854	        val avatarTopY = firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop + avatarTopGap
  1855	        val avatarBounds = Bounds(
  1856	            avatarLeftX,
  1857	            avatarTopY,
  1858	            scaledAvatarSize,
  1859	            scaledAvatarSize
  1860	        )
  1861	        val carouselProgress = mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress()
  1862	        val slideDirection = mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  1863	        val slideDistance = mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize)
  1864	        val dragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
  1865	        val dragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(slideDistance, stageScale)
  1866	
  1867	        drawMockCreateAvatarBubbleShell(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1868	
  1869	        canvas.save()
  1870	        clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  1871	        if (
  1872	            activeMockCreateAvatarDrag &&
  1873	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  1874	            dragDirection != null &&
  1875	            !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
  1876	        ) {
  1877	            val dragSlideDirection = dragDirection.stageDirection
  1878	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1879	                canvas,
  1880	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  1881	                avatarBounds,
  1882	                -dragSlideDirection * dragProgress * slideDistance,
  1883	                stageLeft,
  1884	                stageTop,
  1885	                stageScale,
  1886	                alpha
  1887	            )
  1888	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1889	                canvas,
  1890	                mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(dragDirection),
  1891	                avatarBounds,
  1892	                dragSlideDirection * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance,
  1893	                stageLeft,
  1894	                stageTop,
  1895	                stageScale,
  1896	                alpha
  1897	            )
  1898	        } else if (isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) {
  1899	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1900	                canvas,
  1901	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex,
  1902	                avatarBounds,
  1903	                -slideDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance,
  1904	                stageLeft,
  1905	                stageTop,
  1906	                stageScale,
  1907	                alpha
  1908	            )
  1909	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1910	                canvas,
  1911	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex,
  1912	                avatarBounds,
  1913	                slideDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance,
  1914	                stageLeft,
  1915	                stageTop,
  1916	                stageScale,
  1917	                alpha
  1918	            )
  1919	        } else {
  1920	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1921	                canvas,
  1922	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  1923	                avatarBounds,
  1924	                0f,
  1925	                stageLeft,
  1926	                stageTop,
  1927	                stageScale,
  1928	                alpha
  1929	            )
  1930	        }
  1931	        canvas.restore()
  1932	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  1933	            canvas,
  1934	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  1935	            avatarBounds,
  1936	            stageLeft,
  1937	            stageTop,
  1938	            stageScale,
  1939	            alpha
  1940	        )
  1941	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  1942	            canvas,
  1943	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  1944	            avatarBounds,
  1945	            stageLeft,
  1946	            stageTop,
  1947	            stageScale,
  1948	            alpha
  1949	        )
  1950	    }

  1992	    private fun clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(
  1993	        canvas: Canvas,
  1994	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  1995	        stageLeft: Float,
  1996	        stageTop: Float,
  1997	        stageScale: Float
  1998	    ) {
  1999	        tempPath.reset()
  2000	        tempPath.addCircle(
  2001	            stageLeft + (avatarBounds.x + avatarBounds.w / 2f) * stageScale,
  2002	            stageTop + (avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h / 2f) * stageScale,
  2003	            mockCreateAvatarBubbleInnerRadius(avatarBounds, stageScale),
  2004	            Path.Direction.CW
  2005	        )
  2006	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  2007	    }
  2008	
  2009	    private fun mockCreateAvatarBubbleInnerRadius(avatarBounds: Bounds, stageScale: Float): Float {
  2010	        return (avatarBounds.w / 2f - avatarBounds.w * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO) * stageScale
  2011	    }

  2013	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2014	        canvas: Canvas,
  2015	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2016	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2017	        stageLeft: Float,
  2018	        stageTop: Float,
  2019	        stageScale: Float,
  2020	        alpha: Float
  2021	    ) {
  2022	        val centerX = stageLeft + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2023	        val centerY = stageTop + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2024	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2025	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale / 2f
  2026	        val pressedAlpha = if (pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == direction) 0.62f else 0.9f
  2027	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * pressedAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  2028	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2029	
  2030	        if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  2031	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX - halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2032	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY, centerX + halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2033	        } else {
  2034	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX + halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2035	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY, centerX - halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2036	        }
  2037	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2038	    }

  2040	    private fun drawMockDropdownControl(
  2041	        canvas: Canvas,
  2042	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  2043	        x: Float,
  2044	        y: Float,
  2045	        stageLeft: Float,
  2046	        stageTop: Float,
  2047	        stageScale: Float,
  2048	        alpha: Float,
  2049	        dropdownWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2050	    ) {
  2051	        tempRect.set(
  2052	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2053	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2054	            stageLeft + (x + dropdownWidth) * stageScale,
  2055	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2056	        )
  2057	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2058	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2059	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2060	
  2061	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2062	        formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  2063	        formInputPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2064	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2065	        val textInset = mockDropdownTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2066	        val chevronInset = mockDropdownChevronInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2067	        val maxTextWidth = (dropdownWidth * stageScale - textInset - chevronInset * 2f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2068	        canvas.drawText(
  2069	            trailingFittingText(mockDropdownDisplayText(dropdown), maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  2070	            tempRect.left + textInset,
  2071	            tempRect.centerY() - (formInputPaint.fontMetrics.ascent + formInputPaint.fontMetrics.descent) / 2f,
  2072	            formInputPaint
  2073	        )
  2074	
  2075	        val centerX = tempRect.right - chevronInset
  2076	        val centerY = tempRect.centerY() + 2f * stageScale
  2077	        val halfSize = mockDropdownChevronSize(dropdown) * stageScale / 2f
  2078	        backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2079	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 1.65f * stageScale
  2080	        canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  2081	        canvas.drawLine(centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, centerX + halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  2082	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2083	    }

  2085	    private fun drawMockDropdownOptions(
  2086	        canvas: Canvas,
  2087	        stageLeft: Float,
  2088	        stageTop: Float,
  2089	        stageScale: Float,
  2090	        alpha: Float
  2091	    ) {
  2092	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return
  2093	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return
  2094	
  2095	        val x = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  2096	        val y = mockDropdownOptionsY(
  2097	            dropdown,
  2098	            MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha),
  2099	            mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2100	        )
  2101	        val left = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  2102	        val top = stageTop + y * stageScale
  2103	        val right = stageLeft + (x + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stageScale
  2104	        val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stageScale
  2105	        val optionAlpha = 255
  2106	        val optionTextInset = mockDropdownOptionTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2107	        val scrollbarReservedWidth = if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2108	            (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stageScale
  2109	        } else {
  2110	            0f
  2111	        }
  2112	        val maxTextWidth = (right - left - optionTextInset - scrollbarReservedWidth).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2113	
  2114	        val visibleOptions = mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown)
  2115	        visibleOptions.forEachIndexed { index, option ->
  2116	            val optionTop = top + index * optionHeight
  2117	            val optionBottom = optionTop + optionHeight
  2118	            val fillColor = if (option == mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown)) {
  2119	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 76, 12, 94)
  2120	            } else {
  2121	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 14, 2, 18)
  2122	            }
  2123	            tempRect.set(left, optionTop, right, optionBottom)
  2124	            formFieldFillPaint.color = fillColor
  2125	            formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(optionAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2126	            formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.2f * stageScale
  2127	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldFillPaint)
  2128	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2129	
  2130	            formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownOptionTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  2131	            formInputPaint.alpha = optionAlpha
  2132	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2133	            val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2134	            canvas.drawText(
  2135	                trailingFittingText(option, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  2136	                left + optionTextInset,
  2137	                (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  2138	                formInputPaint
  2139	            )
  2140	        }
  2141	
  2142	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2143	            drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  2144	                canvas,
  2145	                dropdown,
  2146	                top,
  2147	                right,
  2148	                optionHeight * visibleOptions.size,
  2149	                stageScale,
  2150	                optionAlpha
  2151	            )
  2152	        }
  2153	    }
  2154	
  2155	    private fun drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  2156	        canvas: Canvas,
  2157	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  2158	        top: Float,
  2159	        right: Float,
  2160	        dropdownHeight: Float,
  2161	        stageScale: Float,
  2162	        alpha: Int
  2163	    ) {
  2164	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  2165	        val visibleCount = mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  2166	        val maxOffset = mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown)
  2167	        if (options.isEmpty() || maxOffset <= 0) return
  2168	
  2169	        val trackRight = right - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * stageScale
  2170	        val trackLeft = trackRight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale
  2171	        val trackTop = top + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  2172	        val trackBottom = top + dropdownHeight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  2173	        val trackHeight = (trackBottom - trackTop).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2174	        val thumbHeight = maxOf(14f * stageScale, trackHeight * visibleCount / options.size)
  2175	            .coerceAtMost(trackHeight)
  2176	        val thumbProgress = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown) / maxOffset.toFloat()
  2177	        val thumbTop = trackTop + (trackHeight - thumbHeight) * thumbProgress
  2178	        val radius = MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2179	
  2180	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, trackTop, trackRight, trackBottom)
  2181	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.28f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2182	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2183	
  2184	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, thumbTop, trackRight, thumbTop + thumbHeight)
  2185	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.78f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2186	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2187	    }

  2189	    private fun drawMockSignInField(
  2190	        canvas: Canvas,
  2191	        field: MockSignInField,
  2192	        x: Float,
  2193	        y: Float,
  2194	        stageLeft: Float,
  2195	        stageTop: Float,
  2196	        stageScale: Float,
  2197	        alpha: Float,
  2198	        fieldWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2199	    ) {
  2200	        tempRect.set(
  2201	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2202	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2203	            stageLeft + (x + fieldWidth) * stageScale,
  2204	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2205	        )
  2206	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2207	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2208	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2209	
  2210	        val label = mockSignInFieldLabel(field)
  2211	        val labelProgress = mockSignInFieldLabelProgress(field)
  2212	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2213	        formLabelPaint.textSize = lerpFloat(
  2214	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE,
  2215	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * MOCK_FORM_FLOATING_LABEL_SCALE,
  2216	            labelProgress
  2217	        ) * stageScale
  2218	        formLabelPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2219	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2220	        val metrics = formLabelPaint.fontMetrics
  2221	        val placeholderBaselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2222	        val floatingBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_TOP_INSET_Y * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  2223	        val labelX = lerpFloat(
  2224	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale,
  2225	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_INSET_X * stageScale,
  2226	            labelProgress
  2227	        )
  2228	        val labelBaselineY = lerpFloat(placeholderBaselineY, floatingBaselineY, labelProgress)
  2229	        canvas.drawText(
  2230	            label,
  2231	            labelX,
  2232	            labelBaselineY,
  2233	            formLabelPaint
  2234	        )
  2235	
  2236	        val fieldText = mockSignInFieldDisplayText(field)
  2237	        val shouldDrawInput = fieldText.isNotEmpty() || focusedMockField == field
  2238	        if (shouldDrawInput) {
  2239	            val inputAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * labelProgress * 255f).roundToInt()
  2240	            formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2241	            formInputPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  2242	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2243	            formCaretPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  2244	
  2245	            val inputX = tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale
  2246	            val inputBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_INPUT_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * stageScale
  2247	            val maxInputWidth = fieldWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  2248	            val visibleText = trailingFittingText(fieldText, maxInputWidth, formInputPaint)
  2249	            canvas.drawText(visibleText, inputX, inputBaselineY, formInputPaint)
  2250	
  2251	            if (focusedMockField == field) {
  2252	                val caretX = inputX + formInputPaint.measureText(visibleText) + 2f * stageScale
  2253	                val inputMetrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2254	                canvas.drawLine(
  2255	                    caretX,
  2256	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.ascent,
  2257	                    caretX,
  2258	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.descent,
  2259	                    formCaretPaint
  2260	                )
  2261	            }
  2262	        }
  2263	    }
