  2265	    private fun drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  2266	        canvas: Canvas,
  2267	        x: Float,
  2268	        y: Float,
  2269	        stageLeft: Float,
  2270	        stageTop: Float,
  2271	        stageScale: Float,
  2272	        alpha: Float
  2273	    ) {
  2274	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2275	        val boxLeft = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  2276	        val boxTop = stageTop + y * stageScale
  2277	        val boxRight = boxLeft + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE * stageScale
  2278	        val boxBottom = boxTop + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE * stageScale
  2279	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked || pressedRememberMe) 46f else 18f).roundToInt()
  2280	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked) 220f else 168f).roundToInt()
  2281	
  2282	        rememberMeBoxFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2283	        rememberMeBoxStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2284	        rememberMeBoxStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.7f * stageScale
  2285	        tempRect.set(boxLeft, boxTop, boxRight, boxBottom)
  2286	        val radius = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_RADIUS * stageScale
  2287	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxFillPaint)
  2288	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxStrokePaint)
  2289	
  2290	        if (rememberMeChecked) {
  2291	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2292	            rememberMeCheckPaint.strokeWidth = 2.45f * stageScale
  2293	            canvas.drawLine(
  2294	                boxLeft + 5.3f * stageScale,
  2295	                boxTop + 11.8f * stageScale,
  2296	                boxLeft + 9.1f * stageScale,
  2297	                boxTop + 15.8f * stageScale,
  2298	                rememberMeCheckPaint
  2299	            )
  2300	            canvas.drawLine(
  2301	                boxLeft + 9.1f * stageScale,
  2302	                boxTop + 15.8f * stageScale,
  2303	                boxLeft + 17.2f * stageScale,
  2304	                boxTop + 6.9f * stageScale,
  2305	                rememberMeCheckPaint
  2306	            )
  2307	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = 255
  2308	        }
  2309	
  2310	        rememberMeTextPaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2311	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2312	        rememberMeTextPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2313	        val metrics = rememberMeTextPaint.fontMetrics
  2314	        val baselineY = (boxTop + boxBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2315	        canvas.drawText(
  2316	            MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT,
  2317	            boxRight + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_LABEL_GAP * stageScale,
  2318	            baselineY,
  2319	            rememberMeTextPaint
  2320	        )
  2321	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = 255
  2322	    }

  2324	    private fun drawBackChevron(
  2325	        canvas: Canvas,
  2326	        centerX: Float,
  2327	        topY: Float,
  2328	        stageLeft: Float,
  2329	        stageTop: Float,
  2330	        stageScale: Float,
  2331	        alpha: Float
  2332	    ) {
  2333	        val chevronAlpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (pressedBackChevron) 0.68f else 0.92f
  2334	        val center = stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  2335	        val top = stageTop + topY * stageScale
  2336	        val bottom = top + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale
  2337	        val halfWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2338	
  2339	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (chevronAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  2340	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2341	        canvas.drawLine(center - halfWidth, top, center, bottom, backChevronPaint)
  2342	        canvas.drawLine(center, bottom, center + halfWidth, top, backChevronPaint)
  2343	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2344	    }

  2346	    private fun decode(resId: Int): Bitmap {
  2347	        return BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, resId, bitmapOptions)
  2348	    }
  2349	
  2350	    private fun createAvatarCharacterBitmap(
  2351	        source: Bitmap,
  2352	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean = false
  2353	    ): Bitmap {
  2354	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  2355	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  2356	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  2357	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2358	
  2359	        val mask = createAvatarCharacterMask(pixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, includeDarkSaturatedPixels)
  2360	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  2361	            if (mask[index]) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  2362	        }
  2363	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  2364	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2365	        }
  2366	    }
  2367	
  2368	    private fun createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  2369	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  2370	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  2371	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  2372	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2373	
  2374	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  2375	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  2376	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) * (0.5f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO)
  2377	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  2378	
  2379	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  2380	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  2381	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  2382	            val dx = x - centerX
  2383	            val dy = y - centerY
  2384	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy < innerRadiusSquared) {
  2385	                Color.TRANSPARENT
  2386	            } else {
  2387	                pixels[index]
  2388	            }
  2389	        }
  2390	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  2391	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2392	        }
  2393	    }
  2394	
  2395	    private fun createAvatarCharacterMask(
  2396	        pixels: IntArray,
  2397	        bitmapWidth: Int,
  2398	        bitmapHeight: Int,
  2399	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean
  2400	    ): BooleanArray {
  2401	        val coloredPixels = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  2402	        pixels.forEachIndexed { index, pixel ->
  2403	            coloredPixels[index] = isColorfulAvatarPixel(pixel, includeDarkSaturatedPixels)
  2404	        }
  2405	
  2406	        val mask = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  2407	        val outlineRadius = 7
  2408	        for (y in 0 until bitmapHeight) {
  2409	            for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  2410	                val index = y * bitmapWidth + x
  2411	                if (!coloredPixels[index]) continue
  2412	
  2413	                mask[index] = true
  2414	                for (dy in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  2415	                    val sampleY = y + dy
  2416	                    if (sampleY !in 0 until bitmapHeight) continue
  2417	                    for (dx in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  2418	                        val sampleX = x + dx
  2419	                        if (sampleX !in 0 until bitmapWidth) continue
  2420	                        val sampleIndex = sampleY * bitmapWidth + sampleX
  2421	                        val samplePixel = pixels[sampleIndex]
  2422	                        val sampleAlpha = Color.alpha(samplePixel)
  2423	                        val sampleMax = maxOf(
  2424	                            Color.red(samplePixel),
  2425	                            Color.green(samplePixel),
  2426	                            Color.blue(samplePixel)
  2427	                        )
  2428	                        if (sampleAlpha > 16 && sampleMax <= 44) {
  2429	                            mask[sampleIndex] = true
  2430	                        }
  2431	                    }
  2432	                }
  2433	            }
  2434	        }
  2435	        return mask
  2436	    }
  2437	
  2438	    private fun isColorfulAvatarPixel(
  2439	        pixel: Int,
  2440	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean
  2441	    ): Boolean {
  2442	        val alpha = Color.alpha(pixel)
  2443	        val red = Color.red(pixel)
  2444	        val green = Color.green(pixel)
  2445	        val blue = Color.blue(pixel)
  2446	        val channelMax = maxOf(red, green, blue)
  2447	        val channelMin = minOf(red, green, blue)
  2448	        if (alpha <= 16) return false
  2449	
  2450	        val chroma = channelMax - channelMin
  2451	        return (channelMax > 48 && chroma > 22) ||
  2452	            (includeDarkSaturatedPixels && channelMax > 36 && chroma > 14)
  2453	    }
  2454	
  2455	    private fun standardSignInBackgroundForSize(): Bitmap? {
  2456	        return when {
  2457	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) &&
  2458	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) -> standardLandscapeBackground
  2459	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) &&
  2460	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) -> standardPortraitBackground
  2461	            else -> null
  2462	        }
  2463	    }
  2464	
  2465	    private fun isSizeCloseTo(actual: Int, expected: Int): Boolean {
  2466	        return abs(actual - expected) <= STANDARD_TABLET_SIZE_TOLERANCE
  2467	    }

  2473	    private fun settledAvatarHitProfile(x: Float, y: Float): CinerificProfile? {
  2474	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) return null
  2475	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return null
  2476	
  2477	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  2478	
  2479	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  2480	        return FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.firstOrNull { target ->
  2481	            val bounds = target.bounds
  2482	            val centerX = stage.left + (bounds.x + bounds.w / 2f) * stage.scale
  2483	            val centerY = stage.top + (bounds.y + stackShiftY + bounds.h / 2f) * stage.scale
  2484	            val radius = min(bounds.w, bounds.h) * stage.scale / 2f
  2485	            val dx = x - centerX
  2486	            val dy = y - centerY
  2487	            dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius
  2488	        }?.profile
  2489	    }

  2647	    private fun updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  2648	        if (!activeMockCreateAvatarDrag) return false
  2649	
  2650	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  2651	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockTouchDownY
  2652	        if (
  2653	            !mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  2654	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > touchSlop &&
  2655	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > abs(dragDeltaY)
  2656	        ) {
  2657	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = true
  2658	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  2659	        }
  2660	
  2661	        if (mockCreateAvatarDragMoved) {
  2662	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  2663	        }
  2664	        return true
  2665	    }

  2667	    private fun mockDropdownOptionHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdownOption? {
  2668	        if (
  2669	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  2670	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  2671	            expandedMockDropdown == null
  2672	        ) {
  2673	            return null
  2674	        }
  2675	
  2676	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return null
  2677	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  2678	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  2679	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  2680	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2681	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  2682	        val yStart = mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2683	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale
  2684	        val right = stage.left + (dropdownX + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stage.scale
  2685	        if (pointerX !in left..right) return null
  2686	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2687	            val scrollbarLeft = right -
  2688	                (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stage.scale
  2689	            if (pointerX >= scrollbarLeft) return null
  2690	        }
  2691	
  2692	        mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown).forEachIndexed { optionIndex, option ->
  2693	            val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown)
  2694	            val top = stage.top + (yStart + optionIndex * optionHeight) * stage.scale
  2695	            val bottom = top + optionHeight * stage.scale
  2696	            if (pointerY in top..bottom) return MockDropdownOption(dropdown, option)
  2697	        }
  2698	        return null
  2699	    }

  2701	    private fun mockDropdownOptionsHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdown? {
  2702	        if (
  2703	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  2704	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  2705	            expandedMockDropdown == null
  2706	        ) {
  2707	            return null
  2708	        }
  2709	
  2710	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return null
  2711	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  2712	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  2713	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  2714	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2715	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  2716	        val yStart = mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2717	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale
  2718	        val right = stage.left + (dropdownX + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stage.scale
  2719	        val top = stage.top + yStart * stage.scale
  2720	        val bottom = top + mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown) * mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale
  2721	        return if (pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom) dropdown else null
  2722	    }
  2723	
  2724	    private fun updateMockDropdownScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  2725	        val dropdown = activeMockDropdownScroll ?: return false
  2726	        if (expandedMockDropdown != dropdown || !mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) return false
  2727	
  2728	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  2729	        val optionHeightPx = (mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  2730	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockDropdownScrollStartY
  2731	        if (abs(dragDeltaY) > touchSlop) {
  2732	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = true
  2733	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  2734	        }
  2735	
  2736	        if (mockDropdownScrollMoved) {
  2737	            val rowDelta = ((mockDropdownScrollStartY - event.y) / optionHeightPx).roundToInt()
  2738	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, mockDropdownScrollStartOffset + rowDelta)
  2739	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  2740	        }
  2741	        return true
  2742	    }
  2743	
  2744	    private fun mockBackChevronHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {
  2745	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return false
  2746	
  2747	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  2748	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  2749	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  2750	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2751	        val chevronTopY = when (activeMockFlow) {
  2752	            MockAccountFlow.SignIn -> {
  2753	                val lastField = activeMockFields().lastOrNull() ?: return false
  2754	                val lastFieldY = mockActiveFieldY(lastField, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2755	                lastFieldY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TOP_GAP + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE +
  2756	                    MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_TOP_GAP
  2757	            }
  2758	            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> {
  2759	                MOCK_FORM_Y + yOffset + focusShiftY + mockCreateFormHeight() + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_TOP_GAP
  2760	            }
  2761	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> mockForgotPasswordBackChevronTopY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2762	            null -> return false
  2763	        }
  2764	        return mockBackChevronContains(x, y, mockActiveFormCenterX(), chevronTopY, stage)
  2765	    }
  2766	
  2767	    private fun shouldStartMockDragReturn(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  2768	        if (mockDragReturnInProgress || isMockSignInClosing()) return false
  2769	
  2770	        val upwardDragY = mockTouchDownY - event.y
  2771	        if (upwardDragY <= mockBackDragThresholdPx()) return false
  2772	
  2773	        val dragX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  2774	        return upwardDragY > abs(dragX)
  2775	    }
  2776	
  2777	    private fun mockBackDragThresholdPx(): Float {
  2778	        val stageThreshold = currentStageMetrics()?.let { stage ->
  2779	            MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD * stage.scale
  2780	        } ?: MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD
  2781	        return maxOf(stageThreshold, touchSlop * 3f)
  2782	    }

  2795	        val bottom = stage.top + (fieldY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stage.scale

  2797	    }

  2887	    private fun mockCreateAvatarCarouselBounds(
  2888	        columnX: Float,
  2889	        firstFieldY: Float,
  2890	        avatarBounds: Bounds
  2891	    ): Bounds {
  2892	        val centerX = columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f
  2893	        val contentTop = firstFieldY
  2894	        val contentBottom = avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h
  2895	        val contentCenterY = (contentTop + contentBottom) / 2f
  2896	        val chevronSpan = avatarBounds.w +
  2897	            (MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_SIDE_GAP + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH / 2f) * 2f
  2898	        val side = maxOf(chevronSpan, contentBottom - contentTop)
  2899	        return Bounds(
  2900	            centerX - side / 2f,
  2901	            contentCenterY - side / 2f,
  2902	            side,
  2903	            side
  2904	        )
  2905	    }

  2926	    private fun mockCreateAvatarChevronContains(
  2927	        pointerX: Float,

  2929	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2930	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2931	        stage: StageMetrics
  2932	    ): Boolean {

  2938	            pointerY in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)
  2939	    }

  2941	    private fun focusMockSignInField(field: MockSignInField) {
  2942	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = field
  2943	        mockInputDimActiveField = field
  2944	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
  2945	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  2946	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  2947	        if (focusedMockField != field) {
  2948	            activeComposingText = ""
  2949	            focusedMockField = field
  2950	            restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  2951	            restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  2952	        }
  2953	        requestFocus()
  2954	        post {
  2955	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  2956	            inputMethodManager?.restartInput(this)
  2957	            inputMethodManager?.showSoftInput(this, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT)
  2958	        }
  2959	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  2960	    }

  2964	        activeComposingText = ""
  2965	        focusedMockField = null
  2966	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null

  2973	        clearFocus()
  2974	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  2975	    }

  3098	        }

  3100	    }

  3214	        return mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size > mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  3215	    }

  3217	    private fun mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  3218	        return (mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] ?: 0).coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  3219	    }

  3221	    private fun mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  3222	        return (mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size - mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)).coerceAtLeast(0)
  3223	    }

  3230	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)

  3233	    }

  3239	        }

  3241	        val selectedIndex = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).indexOf(mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown))
  3242	        setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, if (selectedIndex >= 0) selectedIndex else 0)
  3243	    }

  3350	    private fun mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress(): Float {
  3351	        val startMillis = forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis ?: return if (
  3352	            forgotPasswordSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent

  3354	            1f

  3359	        return (elapsed / MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SUBMIT_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)

  3369	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null

  3371	        }

  3373	
  3374	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  3375	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null

  3554	    private fun mockLandscapeInputLiftTarget(): Float {
  3555	        if (width <= height) return 0f
  3556	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return 0f
  3557	        return if (
  3558	            focusedMockField != null ||
  3559	            expandedMockDropdown != null ||
  3560	            isSoftKeyboardVisible()
  3561	        ) {
  3562	            1f
  3563	        } else {
  3564	            0f
  3565	        }
  3566	    }
  3567	
  3568	    private fun mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale: Float): Float {
  3569	        if (width <= height || mockSignInStartMillis == null) return 0f
  3570	        val liftMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockLandscapeInputLiftProgress)
  3571	        return MOCK_FORM_LANDSCAPE_FOCUS_SHIFT_Y * stageScale * liftMotion
  3572	    }
  3573	
  3574	    private fun isSoftKeyboardVisible(): Boolean {
  3575	        val rootInsets = ViewCompat.getRootWindowInsets(this)
  3576	        if (rootInsets != null) {
  3577	            val imeInsets = rootInsets.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime())
  3578	            if (rootInsets.isVisible(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime()) || imeInsets.bottom > 0) return true
  3579	        }
  3580	
  3581	        val rootHeight = rootView?.height ?: 0
  3582	        if (rootHeight <= 0) return false
  3583	        getWindowVisibleDisplayFrame(tempWindowRect)
  3584	        val obscuredHeight = rootHeight - tempWindowRect.height()
  3585	        return obscuredHeight > rootHeight * 0.18f
  3586	    }

  3602	            focusedMockField != null ||

  3673	    private fun setMockSignInFieldText(field: MockSignInField, text: String) {
  3674	        val limitedText = text.take(MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS)
  3675	        when (field) {
  3676	            MockSignInField.Username -> usernameText = limitedText
  3677	            MockSignInField.Password -> passwordText = limitedText
  3678	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> confirmPasswordText = limitedText
  3679	            MockSignInField.Email -> emailText = limitedText
  3680	            MockSignInField.Month -> monthText = limitedText
  3681	            MockSignInField.Day -> dayText = limitedText
  3682	            MockSignInField.Year -> yearText = limitedText
  3683	        }
  3684	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword && field == MockSignInField.Email) {
  3685	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  3686	        }
  3687	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  3688	        if (mockInputDimAwaitingField == field) {
  3689	            mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
  3690	        }
  3691	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  3692	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  3693	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  3694	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3695	    }
  3696	
  3697	    private fun appendMockSignInInput(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  3698	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  3699	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  3700	        if (input.isEmpty()) return true
  3701	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  3702	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  3703	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  3704	        val insertedText = input.take(availableChars)
  3705	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + insertedText)
  3706	        activeComposingText = ""
  3707	        return true
  3708	    }
  3709	
  3710	    private fun replaceMockSignInComposingText(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  3711	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  3712	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  3713	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  3714	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  3715	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  3716	        val composingText = input.take(availableChars)
  3717	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + composingText)
  3718	        activeComposingText = composingText
  3719	        return true
  3720	    }
  3721	
  3722	    private fun finishMockSignInComposingText(): Boolean {
  3723	        activeComposingText = ""
  3724	        return true
  3725	    }
  3726	
  3727	    private fun deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength: Int): Boolean {
  3728	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  3729	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  3730	        if (currentText.isEmpty()) {
  3731	            activeComposingText = ""
  3732	            return true
  3733	        }
  3734	        val deleteCount = beforeLength.coerceAtLeast(1).coerceAtMost(currentText.length)
  3735	        activeComposingText = ""
  3736	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, currentText.dropLast(deleteCount))
  3737	        return true
  3738	    }
  3739	
  3740	    private fun handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  3741	        activeComposingText = ""
  3742	        val fields = activeMockFields()
  3743	        val fieldIndex = fields.indexOf(focusedMockField)
  3744	        if (fieldIndex >= 0 && fieldIndex < fields.lastIndex && actionCode != EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE) {
  3745	            focusMockSignInField(fields[fieldIndex + 1])
  3746	        } else if (
  3747	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  3748	            focusedMockField == MockSignInField.Email &&
  3749	            mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  3750	        ) {
  3751	            startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  3752	        } else {
  3753	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  3754	            inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  3755	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  3756	        }
  3757	        return true
  3758	    }
  3759	
  3760	    private fun handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  3761	        if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  3762	        return when (keyCode) {
  3763	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  3764	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  3765	            else -> {
  3766	                val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  3767	                typedCharacter?.let(::appendMockSignInInput) ?: false
  3768	            }
  3769	        }
  3770	    }
  3771	
  3772	    private fun sanitizeMockSignInInput(input: String): String {
  3773	        return buildString {
  3774	            input.forEach { character ->
  3775	                if (character != '\n' && character != '\r' && !character.isISOControl()) {
  3776	                    append(character)
  3777	                }
  3778	            }
  3779	        }
  3780	    }
  3781	
  3782	    private fun trailingFittingText(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): String {
  3783	        if (paint.measureText(text) <= maxWidth) return text
  3784	
  3785	        var startIndex = 0
  3786	        while (startIndex < text.length && paint.measureText(text.substring(startIndex)) > maxWidth) {
  3787	            startIndex += 1
  3788	        }
  3789	        return text.substring(startIndex)
  3790	    }
  3791	
  3792	    private fun wrappedTextLines(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): List<String> {
  3793	        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
  3794	        text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n")).forEachIndexed { paragraphIndex, paragraph ->
  3795	            if (paragraphIndex > 0) {
  3796	                lines += ""
  3797	            }
  3798	
  3799	            var currentLine = ""
  3800	            paragraph.replace('\n', ' ')
  3801	                .split(Regex("\\s+"))
  3802	                .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
  3803	                .forEach { word ->
  3804	                    val candidateLine = if (currentLine.isEmpty()) word else "$currentLine $word"
  3805	                    if (currentLine.isEmpty() || paint.measureText(candidateLine) <= maxWidth) {
  3806	                        currentLine = candidateLine
  3807	                    } else {
  3808	                        lines += currentLine
  3809	                        currentLine = word
  3810	                    }
  3811	                }
  3812	            if (currentLine.isNotEmpty()) {
  3813	                lines += currentLine
  3814	            }
  3815	        }
  3816	        return lines
  3817	    }
  3818	
  3819	    private fun moveToward(current: Float, target: Float, amount: Float): Float {
  3820	        return when {
  3821	            current < target -> (current + amount).coerceAtMost(target)
  3822	            current > target -> (current - amount).coerceAtLeast(target)
  3823	            else -> current
  3824	        }
  3825	    }
  3826	
  3827	    private fun currentStageMetrics(): StageMetrics? {
  3828	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return null
  3829	
  3830	        val stageScale = min(width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  3831	        return StageMetrics(
  3832	            left = (width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f,
  3833	            top = (height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f + mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale),
  3834	            scale = stageScale
  3835	        )
  3836	    }

  3863	    private fun currentIntroSnapshot(): CinerificIntroSnapshot {
  3864	        val activeFlowName = activeMockFlow
  3865	            ?.takeIf { mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress > 0f }
  3866	            ?.name
  3867	            .orEmpty()
  3868	        return CinerificIntroSnapshot(

  3873	            emailText = emailText,

  3877	            subscriptionTierText = subscriptionTierText,

  3879	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName = forgotPasswordSubmissionState.name,

  3885	    private fun notifyIntroSnapshotChanged() {
  3886	        val snapshot = currentIntroSnapshot()
  3887	        if (appliedIntroSnapshot == snapshot) return
  3888	
  3889	        appliedIntroSnapshot = snapshot
  3890	        onIntroSnapshotChanged?.invoke(snapshot)
  3891	    }

  3893	    private fun openMockSignInScreen() {
  3894	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 1f) return
  3895	        val currentProgress = mockSignInProgress()
  3896	        activeMockFlow = MockAccountFlow.SignIn
  3897	        mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  3898	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = currentProgress
  3899	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  3900	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  3901	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3902	    }

  3907	        activeMockFlow = MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
  3908	        mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  3909	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = currentProgress
  3910	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  3911	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()

  3913	    }

  3936	        pressedMockDropdown = null
  3937	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null

  3943	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null

  3949	        dayText = MOCK_FORM_DAY_TEXT

  3965	        inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)

  3973	
  3974	    private fun mockSignInProgress(): Float {
  3975	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return 0f
  3976	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  3977	        val progress = (elapsed / mockSignInTransitionDurationMs().toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3978	        return lerpFloat(
  3979	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress,
  3980	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress,
  3981	            progress
  3982	        )
  3983	    }
  3984	
  3985	    private fun isMockSignInAnimating(): Boolean {
  3986	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return false
  3987	        return SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()
  3988	    }
  3989	
  3990	    private fun isMockSignInClosing(): Boolean {
  3991	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f
  3992	    }
  3993	
  3994	    private fun mockSignInTransitionDurationMs(): Long {
  3995	        val distance = abs(mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress - mockSignInTransitionStartProgress)
  3996	        return (MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS * distance)
  3997	            .roundToInt()
  3998	            .coerceAtLeast(1)
  3999	            .toLong()
  4000	    }
  4001	
  4002	    private fun finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded() {
  4003	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return
  4004	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()) return
  4005	
  4006	        if (mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f) {
  4007	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
  4008	            activeMockFlow = null
  4009	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
  4010	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
  4011	            pressedMockField = null
  4012	            pressedRememberMe = false
  4013	            pressedBackChevron = false
  4014	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  4015	            pressedMockDropdown = null
  4016	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  4017	            pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  4018	            pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  4019	            activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  4020	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  4021	            mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  4022	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  4023	            resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  4024	            notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  4025	            return
  4026	        }
  4027	
  4028	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
  4029	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  4030	    }

  4032	    private inner class MockSignInInputConnection : BaseInputConnection(this@CinerificIntroView, false) {
  4033	        override fun commitText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  4034	            return appendMockSignInInput(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  4035	        }
  4036	
  4037	        override fun setComposingText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  4038	            return replaceMockSignInComposingText(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  4039	        }
  4040	
  4041	        override fun finishComposingText(): Boolean {
  4042	            return finishMockSignInComposingText()
  4043	        }
  4044	
  4045	        override fun deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
  4046	            return deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength)
  4047	        }
  4048	
  4049	        override fun deleteSurroundingTextInCodePoints(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
  4050	            return deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength, afterLength)
  4051	        }
  4052	
  4053	        override fun getTextBeforeCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  4054	            val text = focusedMockField?.let(::mockSignInFieldText).orEmpty()
  4055	            return text.takeLast(n.coerceAtLeast(0))
  4056	        }
  4057	
  4058	        override fun getTextAfterCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  4059	            return ""
  4060	        }
  4061	
  4062	        override fun getSelectedText(flags: Int): CharSequence {
  4063	            return ""
  4064	        }
  4065	
  4066	        override fun performEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  4067	            return handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode)
  4068	        }
  4069	
  4070	        override fun sendKeyEvent(event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  4071	            if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  4072	            return when (event.keyCode) {
  4073	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  4074	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  4075	                else -> {
  4076	                    val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  4077	                    if (typedCharacter != null) {
  4078	                        appendMockSignInInput(typedCharacter)
  4079	                    } else {
  4080	                        super.sendKeyEvent(event)
  4081	                    }
  4082	                }
  4083	            }
  4084	        }
  4085	    }
  4086	}

  4229	    val left: Float,
  4230	    val top: Float,
  4231	    val scale: Float
  4232	)
  4233	
  4234	private data class Bounds(
  4235	    val x: Float,
  4236	    val y: Float,
  4237	    val w: Float,
  4238	    val h: Float
  4239	)
  4240	
  4241	private inline fun <reified T : Enum<T>> enumValueOrNull(name: String): T? {
  4242	    return runCatching { enumValueOf<T>(name) }.getOrNull()
  4243	}
  4244	
  4245	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  4246	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  4247	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4248	}
  4249	
  4250	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  4251	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  4252	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4253	}
  4254	
  4255	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  4256	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  4257	}
  4258	
  4259	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  4260	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  4261	}
  4262	
  4263	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  4264	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4265	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  4266	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  4267	    return base + bounce
  4268	}
  4269	
  4270	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  4271	
  4272	private fun lerpBounds(start: Bounds, end: Bounds, amount: Float) = Bounds(
  4273	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  4274	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  4275	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  4276	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  4277	)
  4278	
  4279	private fun offsetBounds(bounds: Bounds, yOffset: Float) = Bounds(
  4280	    x = bounds.x,
  4281	    y = bounds.y + yOffset,
  4282	    w = bounds.w,
  4283	    h = bounds.h
  4284	)
  4285	
  4286	private fun lerpColor(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  4287	    val t = amount.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4288	    return Color.argb(
  4289	        lerpInt(Color.alpha(start), Color.alpha(end), t),
  4290	        lerpInt(Color.red(start), Color.red(end), t),
  4291	        lerpInt(Color.green(start), Color.green(end), t),
  4292	        lerpInt(Color.blue(start), Color.blue(end), t)
  4293	    )
  4294	}
  4295	
  4296	private fun lerpInt(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  4297	    return (start + (end - start) * amount).roundToInt()
  4298	}
  4299	
  4300	private fun combineBitmaps(base: Bitmap, overlay: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  4301	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(base.width, base.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  4302	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  4303	    val paint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  4304	    canvas.drawBitmap(base, 0f, 0f, paint)
  4305	    canvas.drawBitmap(overlay, 0f, 0f, paint)
  4306	    return output
  4307	}
  4308	
  4309	private fun circularBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  4310	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(source.width, source.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  4311	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  4312	    val rect = RectF(0f, 0f, source.width.toFloat(), source.height.toFloat())
  4313	    val maskPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
  4314	    val imagePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  4315	    canvas.drawOval(rect, maskPaint)
  4316	    imagePaint.xfermode = PorterDuffXfermode(PorterDuff.Mode.SRC_IN)
  4317	    canvas.drawBitmap(source, null, rect, imagePaint)
  4318	    imagePaint.xfermode = null
  4319	    return output
  4320	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Cinerific
     2	
     3	Local Android project for the `prahlin/cinerific` GitHub repo.
     4	
     5	This is a first Kotlin / Jetpack Compose scaffold based on the Figma intro and sign-in frames. The current app opens as a tablet-friendly full-screen frame preview with:
     6	
     7	- Intro frame 1
     8	- Animated intro frame 2
     9	- Promo/logo intro frame 3
    10	- Sign-in/loading frame 4
    11	
    12	## Stack
    13	
    14	- Kotlin
    15	- Jetpack Compose Material 3
    16	- Android Gradle Plugin 8.5.x
    17	- Gradle 8.7
    18	
    19	## Run it
    20	
    21	Open the project in Android Studio and run `app`, or use the Gradle wrapper:
    22	
    23	```bash
    24	./gradlew :app:assembleDebug
    25	```
    26	
    27	The project pins Gradle to Android Studio's bundled JBR in `gradle.properties`, so command-line builds do not accidentally use an unsupported system JDK.
    28	
    29	From VS Code, run the default build task `Android: assemble debug`. There are also tasks for `Android: install debug` and `Android: launch Cinerific` when an emulator or device is connected.
    30	
    31	## Notes for the first Kotlin pass
    32	
    33	- The layout is intentionally simple and adaptive for tablets.
    34	- The current visuals are Compose approximations of the Figma frames and are meant to be replaced with production assets screen by screen.
    35	- The local project folder is the same path as the GitHub remote: `prahlin/cinerific`.

============================================================
FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/cinerific_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Cinerific project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Cinerific Android PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you don't understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	
    28	
    29	============================================================
    30	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    31	============================================================
    32	
    33	This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    34	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    35	
    36	The snapshot intentionally ignores:
    37	- .git
    38	- .gradle
    39	- .idea
    40	- app/build
    41	- build
    42	- .kotlin
    43	- .tmp
    44	- .cache
    45	- .DS_Store
    46	- local.properties
    47	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    48	- *.jks
    49	- *.keystore
    50	
    51	Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    52	
    53	
    54	============================================================
    55	PROJECT FILE TREE
    56	============================================================
    57	
    58	EOF
    59	}
    60	
    61	print_file() {
    62	  local file="$1"
    63	
    64	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    65	    {
    66	      echo ""
    67	      echo ""
    68	      echo "============================================================"
    69	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    70	      echo "============================================================"
    71	      echo ""
    72	      nl -ba "$file"
    73	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    74	  fi
    75	}
    76	
    77	print_binary_inventory() {
    78	  local title="$1"
    79	  local dir="$2"
    80	
    81	  {
    82	    echo ""
    83	    echo ""
    84	    echo "============================================================"
    85	    echo "$title"
    86	    echo "============================================================"
    87	    echo ""
    88	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    89	
    90	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
    91	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    92	    return
    93	  fi
    94	
    95	  find "$dir" -type f \
    96	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
    97	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
    98	    ! -name "*.jks" \
    99	    ! -name "*.keystore" \
   100	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   101	    local size
   102	    local modified
   103	    size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   104	    modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   105	    printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   106	  done
   107	}
   108	
   109	write_snapshot() {
   110	  write_header
   111	
   112	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" \
   113	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git" -prune -o \
   114	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.gradle" -prune -o \
   115	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.idea" -prune -o \
   116	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build" -prune -o \
   117	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/build" -prune -o \
   118	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.kotlin" -prune -o \
   119	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.tmp" -prune -o \
   120	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.cache" -prune -o \
   121	    -name ".DS_Store" -prune -o \
   122	    -name "local.properties" -prune -o \
   123	    -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" -prune -o \
   124	    -name "*.jks" -prune -o \
   125	    -name "*.keystore" -prune -o \
   126	    -print | sort >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   127	
   128	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   129	
   130	
   131	============================================================
   132	FILE CONTENTS — ANDROID SOURCE
   133	============================================================
   134	
   135	EOF
   136	
   137	  for file in \
   138	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml" \
   139	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/MainActivity.kt" \
   140	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificApp.kt" \
   141	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificIntroView.kt" \
   142	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/theme/Theme.kt" \
   143	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml" \
   144	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/themes.xml" \
   145	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-night/themes.xml" \
   146	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-v31/themes.xml" \
   147	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/splash_transparent.xml" \
   148	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_background.xml" \
   149	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_foreground.xml" \
   150	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher.xml" \
   151	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher_round.xml"
   152	  do
   153	    print_file "$file"
   154	  done
   155	
   156	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   157	
   158	
   159	============================================================
   160	FILE CONTENTS — ROOT / TOOLING
   161	============================================================
   162	
   163	EOF
   164	
   165	  for file in \
   166	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.gitignore" \
   167	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.github/copilot-instructions.md" \
   168	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/mcp.json" \
   169	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/tasks.json" \
   170	    "$PROJECT_DIR/README.md" \
   171	    "$PROJECT_DIR/build.gradle.kts" \
   172	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build.gradle.kts" \
   173	    "$PROJECT_DIR/settings.gradle.kts" \
   174	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle.properties" \
   175	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties" \
   176	    "$PROJECT_DIR/read_project.sh"
   177	  do
   178	    print_file "$file"
   179	  done
   180	
   181	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY — DRAWABLE-NODPI" "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable-nodpi"
   182	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / SCREENSHOT INVENTORY" "$PROJECT_DIR/screenshots"
   183	
   184	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   185	
   186	
   187	============================================================
   188	NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
   189	============================================================
   190	
   191	The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
   192	- .git
   193	- .gradle
   194	- .idea
   195	- app/build
   196	- build
   197	- .kotlin
   198	- .tmp
   199	- .cache
   200	- .DS_Store
   201	- local.properties
   202	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
   203	- *.jks
   204	- *.keystore
   205	
   206	The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
   207	
   208	EOF
   209	}
   210	
   211	write_snapshot
   212	
   213	echo "Done."
   214	echo ""
   215	echo "Snapshot created here:"
   216	echo "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   217	echo ""
   218	echo "Open it with:"
   219	echo "open \"$OUTPUT_FILE\""

============================================================
NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
============================================================

The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.jks
- *.keystore

The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
